# What changed, why it matters, and what comes next

Generated from reviewed evidence data. Entries describe actual reviewed changes; an unrelated repository push does not add a capability milestone.

[Current tracker](../../README.md#progress-in-numbers) · [Maintenance](TRACKER.md)

## 2026-10-30 · original-ide-play-policy-portable-01

**Evidence class:** synthetic-linux. **Runtime capability advanced:** no.

**Changed:** Explicit original IDE published-play policies select fixed \(22\), reactive \(23\) or patrol/V2 \(24\) for receipt-bound Playtest and Export\+Play. Owned selection is captured before callbacks/export scheduling. A live change suppresses pending handoff without undoing completed geometry publication. The joined checked-export receipt remains geometry authority; no policy receipt is fabricated. Independent existing-file play remains policy 22; historical reports retain the actual policy.

**Why:** Modeled-native tests exercised admitted originally authored fixtures, real Open/edit/export/render workers and joins, checked geometry/material outputs, strict loaders/session/modal loop, dirty edits, retained images and new test saves. Optimized author/independent cohorts and separately executed O0 ASan/UBSan cohorts passed with leaks disabled; legacy checks retained existing-file policy 22 under UI policy 24. Genuine stale-policy callback RED exposed a wrongly excluded live policy change during Export\+Play settlement; a narrow correction passed the independent cases. Earlier fixed/512-byte output and missing capture/report REDs, reviewer setup failure and corrected post-scheduling Image oracle remain distinct.

**Next:** Policies 22/23 produced 512-byte test saves and policy 24 produced 528-byte V2 with moved-target state; no owner saves were used. Three fresh translation units plus three dependency-checked reused objects linked into inert front-door/IDE targets, never run. No Windows GUI/device/audibility, MML fidelity, production startup/gameplay/save, full IDE, provenance, S2 or transfer acceptance. Later unaccepted slices are excluded.

**Evidence:** [source 1](../../docs/status/current-status.md#original-ide-published-play-policy-selection--october-30).

**Evidence class:** synthetic-linux. **Runtime capability advanced:** no.

Independent evidence separates analytic axes/tie/range from explicitly seeded strict-scene/parking boundaries and natural traversal. Baseline behavior RED and setup/strict-compile failures remain preserved. Two Windows targets compiled, linked and passed inert inspection with exact reused dependencies disclosed, never executed.

**Next:** Save layout/version remains unchanged but uses distinct sealed policy identity and strict crossed-policy refusal, with no migration. Cross-policy evidence directly proves owner/state nonadoption, not direct live-pixel comparison. No Windows/user acceptance, general LOS/moving AI, MML fidelity, production game boot, provenance or S2 acceptance follows.

**Evidence:** [source 1](../../docs/status/current-status.md#original-reactive-stationary-guard--october-30).

**Evidence class:** synthetic-linux. **Runtime capability advanced:** no.

## 2026-10-30 · original-shot-audio-portable-01

**Evidence class:** synthetic-linux. **Runtime capability advanced:** no.

**Changed:** An explicitly selected optional original tone supplies one owned cue request for each visible accepted shot. Blank selection is disclosed silent mode; invalid or unreadable selected input refuses before child launch. Busy cue requests are dropped without reversing gameplay.

**Why:** A dedicated worker owns native audio resources. Focused F8 explicitly retries current blocked cleanup; Close waits for proven cleanup and a joined worker while the modeled message pump remains responsive. Optimized/executed sanitizer checks covered accepted/busy/refused shots, device failures, natural completion, pause/close, retry generations and quit propagation. Final focused optional-control reads passed. Earlier source variants and final focused applicability were reviewed separately; compiler resource interruption, harness mistakes and genuine caption/drain/quit/input-refusal REDs and corrections remain preserved.

**Next:** Native targets linked the real audio API but were not run. This is no Windows audibility, visual/accessibility, hardware audio, faithful MML behavior, production game boot, full IDE, provenance or S2 acceptance. Keep existing source-applicability limits and earlier-build Windows evidence distinct.

**Evidence:** [source 1](../../docs/status/current-status.md#original-accepted-shot-audio--october-30).
